.
It’s easy to see that all the entries in the above table
are non-negative.

Again, we have

\displaystyle P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(*,m_{1}^{\prime},*)

\displaystyle=P_{t_{1}}^{\mathrm{Mat}}(m_{1}^{\prime})=2^{-m_{1}^{\prime}(m_{1}^{\prime}-t_{1})}\prod_{i=1}^{m_{1}^{\prime}}(1-2^{-i})^{-1}\prod_{i=1}^{m_{1}^{\prime}-t_{1}}(1-2^{-i})^{-1}\prod_{i=1}^{\infty}(1-2^{-i}),

\displaystyle P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(*,*,m_{2}^{\prime})

\displaystyle=P_{t_{2}}^{\mathrm{Mat}}(m_{2}^{\prime})=2^{-m_{2}^{\prime}(m_{2}^{\prime}-t_{2})}\prod_{i=1}^{m_{2}^{\prime}}(1-2^{-i})^{-1}\prod_{i=1}^{m_{2}^{\prime}-t_{2}}(1-2^{-i})^{-1}\prod_{i=1}^{\infty}(1-2^{-i}).

For
P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(*,m_{1}^{\prime},m_{2}^{\prime})
, it’s easy to check
that
P_{(*,m_{1}^{\prime},m_{2}^{\prime})\to(*,m_{1,{\mathrm{new}}}^{\prime},m_{2,{\mathrm{new}}}^{\prime})}^{\mathrm{Alt}}

in the above table
is irreducible and aperiodic, and we have the following result.

Proposition A.13
.

For
m_{1}^{\prime},m_{2}^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geq 0}
such that

m_{1}^{\prime}-m_{2}^{\prime}\geq t_{1}
and
m_{2}^{\prime}-m_{1}^{\prime}\geq t_{2}
,
the following formula

P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(*,m_{1}^{\prime},m_{2}^{\prime})=\frac{\displaystyle 2^{m_{1}^{\prime}m_{2}^{\prime}-m_{1}^{\prime}(m_{1}^{\prime}-t_{1})-m_{2}^{\prime}(m_{2}^{\prime}-t_{2})}\prod_{i=1}^{-t_{1}-t_{2}}(1-2^{-i})}{\displaystyle\prod_{i=1}^{m_{1}^{\prime}}(1-2^{-i})\prod_{i=1}^{m_{1}^{\prime}-m_{2}^{\prime}-t_{1}}(1-2^{-i})\prod_{i=1}^{m_{2}^{\prime}}(1-2^{-i})\prod_{i=1}^{m_{2}^{\prime}-m_{1}^{\prime}-t_{2}}(1-2^{-i})}\prod_{i=1}^{\infty}(1-2^{-i})

gives an equilibrium distribution.

Denote
z:=2^{-m}
, then
for
P_{\mathbf{m}\to\mathbf{m}_{\mathrm{new}}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}

we have the following tables.

m_{\mathrm{new}}=m+2

m^{\prime}_{2,{\mathrm{new}}}

m^{\prime}_{1,{\mathrm{new}}}

m_{1}^{\prime}-1

m_{1}^{\prime}

m_{1}^{\prime}+1

*

m_{2}^{\prime}+1

0

\tau_{2}y_{2}(z-\tau_{1}y_{1}^{2})/2y_{1}

\tau_{1}\tau_{2}y_{1}y_{2}/2

\tau_{2}y_{2}z/2y_{1}

m_{2}^{\prime}

0

(z-\tau_{1}y_{1}^{2})(z-\tau_{2}y_{2}^{2})/2y_{1}y_{2}

\tau_{1}y_{1}(z-\tau_{2}y_{2}^{2})/2y_{2}

z(z-\tau_{2}y_{2}^{2})/2y_{1}y_{2}

m_{2}^{\prime}-1

0

0

0

0

*

0

z(z-\tau_{1}y_{1}^{2})/2y_{1}y_{2}

\tau_{1}y_{1}z/2y_{2}

z^{2}/2y_{1}y_{2}

m_{\mathrm{new}}=m-2

m^{\prime}_{2,{\mathrm{new}}}

m^{\prime}_{1,{\mathrm{new}}}

m_{1}^{\prime}-1

m_{1}^{\prime}

m_{1}^{\prime}+1

*

m_{2}^{\prime}+1

0

0

0

0

m_{2}^{\prime}

(1-y_{1})(y_{2}-z/y_{1})

(y_{1}y_{2}-z)(y_{1}y_{2}-2z)/y_{1}y_{2}

0

(y_{2}-z/y_{1})(1-2z/y_{2})

m_{2}^{\prime}-1

(1-y_{1})(1-y_{2})

(1-y_{2})(y_{1}-z/y_{2})

0

(1-y_{2})(1-z/y_{2})

*

(1-y_{1})(1-z/y_{1})

(y_{1}-z/y_{2})(1-2z/y_{1})

0

1-z/y_{1}-z/y_{2}-z+2z^{2}/y_{1}y_{2}

m_{\mathrm{new}}=m

m^{\prime}_{2,{\mathrm{new}}}

m^{\prime}_{1,{\mathrm{new}}}

m_{1}^{\prime}-1

m_{1}^{\prime}

m_{1}^{\prime}+1

*

m_{2}^{\prime}+1

0

\tau_{2}y_{2}(y_{1}y_{2}-z)/2y_{1}

0

\tau_{2}y_{2}(y_{1}y_{2}-z)/2y_{1}

m_{2}^{\prime}

(1-y_{1})(z-\tau_{1}y_{1}^{2})/y_{1}

\begin{array}[]{c}\tau_{1}y_{1}+\tau_{2}y_{2}-3(\tau_{1}y_{1}^{2}+\tau_{2}y_{2}^{2})/2\\
{}+z(\tau_{1}y_{1}^{2}+\tau_{2}y_{2}^{2}-5z)/2y_{1}y_{2}+3z\end{array}

\tau_{1}y_{1}(y_{1}y_{2}-z)/2y_{2}

\begin{array}[]{c}\tau_{2}y_{2}-3\tau_{2}y_{2}^{2}/2-5z^{2}/2y_{1}y_{2}\\
{}+\tau_{2}y_{2}z/2y_{1}+z/y_{1}+2z\end{array}

m_{2}^{\prime}-1

0

(1-y_{2})(z-\tau_{2}y_{2}^{2})/y_{2}

0

(1-y_{2})(z-\tau_{2}y_{2}^{2})/y_{2}

*

(1-y_{1})(z-\tau_{1}y_{1}^{2})/y_{1}

\begin{array}[]{c}\tau_{1}y_{1}-3\tau_{1}y_{1}^{2}/2-5z^{2}/2y_{1}y_{2}\\
{}+\tau_{1}y_{1}z/2y_{2}+z/y_{2}+2z\end{array}

\tau_{1}y_{1}(y_{1}y_{2}-z)/2y_{2}

z/y_{1}+z/y_{2}+z-5z^{2}/2y_{1}y_{2}

From the proof of Theorem
A.6

(that is, divide
B\to B_{\mathrm{new}}
as two steps)
we know that all the entries in the above table are non-negative.
Now it’s easy to check
that
P_{\mathbf{m}\to\mathbf{m}_{\mathrm{new}}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}

is irreducible and aperiodic, and we have the following result.

Proposition A.14
.

The

P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(m,m_{1}^{\prime},m_{2}^{\prime})=P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(*,m_{1}^{\prime},m_{2}^{\prime})\cdot\lambda_{T,A,B}

gives an equilibrium distribution,
where
T:=-(t_{1}+t_{2})/2\in{\mathbb{Z}}
,
A:=m_{2}^{\prime}-m_{1}^{\prime}-t_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}
,

B:=m-m_{1}^{\prime}-m_{2}^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{Z}}
,
which satisfy
T\geq 0
,
0\leq A\leq 2T
,
0\leq B\leq\min\{A,2T-A\}
,

B\equiv A\,\left(\mathrm{mod}\,2\right)
,
and

\lambda_{T,A,B}=2^{(A-B)(2T-A-B)/2}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{A-B}(2^{B+i}-1)}{\prod_{i=1}^{(A-B)/2}(2^{2i}-1)}\cdot\frac{\prod_{i=(A-B)/2}^{\lfloor A/2\rfloor-1}(2^{2T+2-2A+2i}-1)}{\prod_{i=0}^{\lfloor A/2\rfloor-1}(2^{2T-1-2i}-1)},

which satisfies
\lambda_{T,A,B}=\lambda_{T,2T-A,B}
.

In particular, if
t_{1}\leq 0
,
t_{2}\leq 0
are both even, then

P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(0)=P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(0,0,0)=\prod_{i=1}^{-(t_{1}+t_{2})/2}(1-4^{-i})\prod_{i=1}^{-t_{1}/2}(1-4^{-i})^{-1}\prod_{i=1}^{-t_{2}/2}(1-4^{-i})^{-1}\prod_{i=1}^{\infty}(1-2^{-i}).

Corollary A.15
.

For
m\geq\max\{t_{1},t_{2},0\}
such that
m\equiv t\,\left(\mathrm{mod}\,2\right)
,
the
P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(m)
is positive, equal to the sum of

P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(m,m_{1}^{\prime},m_{2}^{\prime})
over all possible
m_{1}^{\prime}
and
m_{2}^{\prime}
.
For other
m
, we have
P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(m)=0
, in fact,
in this case there is no
\omega\in\Omega_{k}
such that
\corank(B(\omega))=m
.

Corollary A.16
.

If
t_{1}+t_{2}\leq 0
, define the formal power series

F_{0}(X):=\frac{\prod_{j=0}^{\infty}(1+2^{-j}X)}{\prod_{j=0}^{\infty}(1+2^{-j})}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\geq 0}2^{i_{1}t_{1}+i_{2}t_{2}}\cdot\frac{2^{2i_{1}i_{2}}\prod_{j=1}^{i_{1}+i_{2}}(2^{1-j}X-1)}{\prod_{j=1}^{i_{1}}(2^{2j}-1)\prod_{j=1}^{i_{2}}(2^{2j}-1)}\in{\mathbb{R}}[[X]]

and
F(X):=F_{0}(X)+(-1)^{t}F_{0}(-X)\in{\mathbb{R}}[[X]]
.
Then
F(X)
is the generating function of
P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}
:

\sum_{m}P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(m)X^{m}=F(X).

It converges on the whole complex plane.
In particular, for
\xi\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geq 0}
,
the
\xi
-th moment of the size of
\ker(B)
as
B\in\Omega_{k}
,
k\to\infty
is

\sum_{m}P_{t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}(m)2^{\xi\cdot m}=F(2^{\xi})=F_{0}(2^{\xi})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2}\geq 0\\
i_{1}+i_{2}\leq\xi\end{subarray}}2^{i_{1}t_{1}+i_{2}t_{2}}\cdot\frac{\displaystyle 2^{2i_{1}i_{2}}\prod_{j=1}^{\xi}(2^{2j}-1)}{\displaystyle\prod_{j=1}^{i_{1}}(2^{2j}-1)\prod_{j=1}^{i_{2}}(2^{2j}-1)\prod_{j=1}^{\xi-i_{1}-i_{2}}(2^{j}-1)}.

Taking
\xi=1
, the average size of
\ker(B)
as
B\in\Omega_{k}
,
k\to\infty
is
3+2^{t_{1}}+2^{t_{2}}
.

A.3.
Average order of kernel of matrix models

We apply Heath-Brown’s method
[
HB93
]
,
[
HB94
]
(see also
Proposition
3.15
)
to the matrix model
M_{2k+t,\mathbf{t}}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2})
,
to compute the
\omega
-average size of the kernel.

A.3.1.
Type (A) model

For
B=(b_{ij})\in M_{2k+t}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2})
,
let
r:=2k+t

and
V=W={\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{r}
.
For
v\in V
and
w\in W
, define
\iota:[r]\to{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
,

i\mapsto(v_{i};w_{i})
.
For
\lambda\in{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
, let
[r]_{\lambda}
be the preimage of
\lambda
under
\iota
.
Then
w^{\mathrm{T}}Bv=\sum_{(i,j)\in S}b_{ij}

where
S=([r]_{01}\times[r]_{10})\sqcup([r]_{01}\times[r]_{11})\sqcup([r]_{10}\times[r]_{11})
,
hence

\sum_{B\in M_{2k+t}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2})}(-1)^{w^{\mathrm{T}}Bv}=\begin{cases}\#M_{2k+t}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2}),&\text{if }S=\varnothing,\\
0,&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

Note that
S
being empty or not depends only on
\Lambda:=\Im(\iota)
.
When
\Lambda
is fixed, the number of
\iota

such that
\Im(\iota)=\Lambda
is equal to

\#\{[r]\twoheadrightarrow[\#\Lambda]\}=(\#\Lambda)^{r}+O((\#\Lambda-1)^{r})
,
hence only the
\Lambda
with
\#\Lambda\geq 2

and
S=\varnothing

have non-zero contributions
when
k\to\infty
.
Such
\Lambda
are
\{00,01\}
,
\{00,10\}
and
\{00,11\}
.
Therefore

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\big(2^{\corank(B)}\mid B\in M_{2k+t}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2})\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\#M_{2k+t}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2})\cdot\#W}\sum_{v\in V,w\in W}\sum_{B\in M_{2k+t}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2})}(-1)^{w^{\mathrm{T}}Bv}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2^{r}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\Lambda\text{ such that}\\
\#\Lambda\geq 2\text{ and }S=\varnothing\end{subarray}}\#\{[r]\twoheadrightarrow[\#\Lambda]\}+O((1/2)^{r})=3+O((1/4)^{k}).

A.3.2.
Type (B) model

Similar to type (A),
the map
\iota:[r]\to{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
restricts to

\iota_{1}:[k_{1}]\to{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
and
\iota_{2}:[k_{1}+1\mathop{..}r]\to{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
.
For
\ell\in\{1,2\}
and
\lambda\in{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
let
[r]_{\lambda}^{(\ell)}

be the preimage of
\lambda
under
\iota_{\ell}
, then

w^{\mathrm{T}}Bv=\sum_{(i,j)\in S}b_{ij}

where

S=\left(\bigsqcup_{\lambda,\lambda^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}\setminus\{0\},\lambda\neq\lambda^{\prime}}[r]_{\lambda}^{(1)}\times[r]_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{(2)}\right)\sqcup([r]_{01}^{(2)}\times[r]_{10}^{(2)})\sqcup([r]_{01}^{(2)}\times[r]_{11}^{(2)})\sqcup([r]_{10}^{(2)}\times[r]_{11}^{(2)}).

The
S
being empty or not depends only on
\Lambda_{1}:=\Im(\iota_{1})

and
\Lambda_{2}:=\Im(\iota_{2})
,
and only the
\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}
with
\#\Lambda_{1}\cdot\#\Lambda_{2}\geq 4

have non-zero contributions when
k\to\infty
.
Such
\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}
are

\Lambda_{1}=\Lambda_{2}=\{00,01\}
,
\{00,10\}
and
\{00,11\}
;
and
\Lambda_{1}={\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
,
\Lambda_{2}=\{00\}
.
Therefore

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\big(2^{\corank(B)}\mid B\in M_{2k+t,(t_{1})}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2})\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\#M_{2k+t,(t_{1})}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2})\cdot\#W}\sum_{v\in V,w\in W}\sum_{B\in M_{2k+t,(t_{1})}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2})}(-1)^{w^{\mathrm{T}}Bv}

\displaystyle=\frac{3\cdot 2^{k_{1}}\cdot 2^{k_{2}}+4^{k_{1}}}{2^{r}}+O((3/4)^{k})=3+2^{t_{1}}+O((3/4)^{k}).

A.3.3.
Type (C) model

Similar to type (A),
the map
\iota:[r]\to{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
restricts to

\iota_{1}:[k_{1}]\to{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
,

\iota_{2}:[k_{1}+1\mathop{..}k_{1}+k_{2}]\to{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
,
and
\iota_{3}:[k_{1}+k_{2}+1\mathop{..}r]\to{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
.
For
\ell\in\{1,2,3\}
and
\lambda\in{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
let
[r]_{\lambda}^{(\ell)}

be the preimage of
\lambda
under
\iota_{\ell}
, then

w^{\mathrm{T}}Bv=\sum_{(i,j)\in S}b_{ij}

where

S=\left(\bigsqcup_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq\ell<\ell^{\prime}\leq 3\\
\lambda,\lambda^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}\setminus\{0\},\lambda\neq\lambda^{\prime}\end{subarray}}[r]_{\lambda}^{(\ell)}\times[r]_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{(\ell^{\prime})}\right)\sqcup([r]_{01}^{(3)}\times[r]_{10}^{(3)})\sqcup([r]_{01}^{(3)}\times[r]_{11}^{(3)})\sqcup([r]_{10}^{(3)}\times[r]_{11}^{(3)}).

The
S
being empty or not depends only on
\Lambda_{\ell}:=\Im(\iota_{\ell})
,

\ell\in\{1,2,3\}
,
and only the
(\Lambda_{\ell})
with
\#\Lambda_{1}\cdot\#\Lambda_{2}\geq 4

have non-zero contributions when
k\to\infty
.
Such
(\Lambda_{\ell})
are

\Lambda_{3}\subset\Lambda_{1}=\Lambda_{2}=\{00,01\}
,
\{00,10\}
and
\{00,11\}
;

\Lambda_{1}={\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
,
\Lambda_{3}\subset\Lambda_{2}=\{00\}
;
and
\Lambda_{2}={\mathbb{F}}_{2}^{2}
,
\Lambda_{3}\subset\Lambda_{1}=\{00\}
.
Therefore

{\mathbb{E}}\big(2^{\corank(B)}\mid B\in M_{2k+t,(t_{1},t_{2})}^{\mathrm{Alt}}({\mathbb{F}}_{2})\big)=\frac{3\cdot 2^{k_{1}}\cdot 2^{k_{2}}\cdot 2^{k_{3}}+4^{k_{1}}+4^{k_{2}}}{2^{r}}+O((3/4)^{k})=3+2^{t_{1}}+2^{t_{2}}+O((3/4)^{k}).
